Cross_corr function:
def cross_corr(w,p):
  n=len(p); L=2n-1; size=1<<(2*n+ n? need w length L, p length n, full length L+n-1=3n-2). Use size = nextpow2(L+n-1). But we can reduce: We need s_j = ∑_{k=max(0,j)}^{min(L-1,j+n-1)} w_k p_{k-j}. This is cross-correlation. It can be computed by convolution of w and reversed p, segment n-1..2n-2 as derived, with size >= L+n-1. size = nextpow2(3n-2). For n=10000, size=65536 (3n=30000). OK. Return conv(size)[n-1:2n-1].
  Alternative using scipy.signal.fftconvolve maybe C optimized? Use numpy.
  Implementation: wr = np.ascontiguousarray(w); pr = p[::-1]; F_wr=rfft(wr,size); F_pr=rfft(pr,size); conv=irfft(F_wr*F_pr,size)[:3n-2]; return conv[n-1:2n-1]. Need check formula with example small.
Test n=3, p=[a,b,c], w length5 [w0..w4]. p_rev=[c,b,a]. conv = [c w0, c w1+b w0, c w2+b w1+a w0, c w3+b w2+a w1, c w4+b w3+a w2, b w4+a w3, a w4]. Indices n-1=2 to4: idx2=c w2+b w1+a w0. Desired j=0: w0 p? s0=Σ_w w_k p_{k} for k valid 0..2 = w0 a + w1 b + w2 c. Our idx2 = a w0 + b w1 + c w2 correct. idx3 = c w3+b w2+a w1 = w1 a+w2 b+w3 c (k=1..3) yes j=1: Σ w_k p_{k-1}, p0 at k=1 ->w1 a, p1 w2 b,p2 w3 c. idx4 = c w4+b w3+a w2 = w2 a+w3 b+w4 c (j=2). Good.

Adam details:
- grad = p*(cross - mean_cross). This may have tiny components. Use float64.
- m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad; v=b2*v+(1-b2)*grad²
- m_hat=m/(1-b1^t); v_hat=v/(1-b2^t); step = lr*m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+1e-8)
- θ = θ - step
- Re-center: θ -= θ.mean() to prevent drift; maybe clip θ to [-50,50]. But clipping after recenter. If p has small zeros, θ could be -20. This allows q min ~e^{-20}/n ~1e-12.
- But objective doesn't require strict positive; zeros okay. Clip prevents extreme. If good solution requires zero, min value not harmful.
- If some p underflows to 0, cross still ok. Softmax computed from θ clip; if θ all near -50? recenter ensures max~0.

Stalling/restarts:
- Keep best_p and best_H. current_H.
- If current_H > best_H*(1+stall_tol) or no improvement for reset_steps (e.g. 2000), set θ = log(best_p/mean) + noise; noise_scale maybe 0.05*scale. Reset Adam. This explores around best.
- For global exploration, call soft_opt multiple times from different initial p with different seeds/time.

def fourier_objective(coeffs, A, N):
    f = 1.0 + A.dot(coeffs)  # if A shape (D,N), coeffs (D,) -> array
    if f.min()<0: f=f.clip? Clipping non-smooth but okay.
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=0: return 1e3
    p=f/s
    C=fast_conv(p)
    return float(N * C.max())  # H

Actually autocorr A(s)=∫ f(x)f(x+s) = (f * flip f)(t) with t =? Fourier of A is |F|². Our g(t)=∫ f(x)f(t-x) dx = (f*f)(t), Fourier F(ω)^2. If we set h(x)=f(-x)? Not. Yes f-supported positive, fR negative support. So g is cross-correlation of f and reversed. Its Fourier transform is F(ω) * F_rev(ω)=F(ω) * \bar{F(ω)}? For real, Fourier of fR is conjugate? If support? Actually F_rev(ω)=? If fR(x)=f(-x), its FT is \bar{F(ω)} for real f? So FT of g = |F(ω)|²? Wait convolution of f and f_rev has FT F(ω)*F_rev(ω)=F(ω)*conj(F(ω)) = |F|². Yes! Great. Let's verify: g(t)=∫ f(x) f(t-x) dx. Let h(x)=f(-x). Then (f*h)(t)=∫ f(x)h(t-x)=∫ f(x) f(x-t) dx. Not same t? If replace t by -t: (f*h)(-t)=∫ f(x)f(x+t) dx. Our g(t)=∫ f(x)f(t-x). Substitute y=t-x => ∫ f(t-y)f(y)dy = ∫ f(y)f(t-y). This is not ∫ f(x)f(x+t). But if h(x)=f(1? maybe support transformation). Let's do finite: full linear convolution length 2n-1 is symmetric: C_k=C_{2n-2-k}. Autocorrelation of p is rev conv? For infinite sequences, autocorr r(s)=∑ p_i p_{i+s}. For k? Set i=n-1-k? Maybe not.

DFT of linear convolution of f with itself is F(ω)^2, not |F|², unless we work with unwrap relative to center. If we define centered function f_c on [-L,L], convolution at 0 corresponds sum products; Fourier transform of self-convolution is F_c^2. So not necessarily positive definite. Detailed functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def next_pow2(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def fast_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    size=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64), size)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*A, size)
    return C[:2*n-1]

def score_p(p):
    p=np.maximum(np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64),0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf, np.inf, np.zeros(0)
    p=p/s
    C=fast_conv(p)
    maxC=C.max()
    return float(2.0*len(p)*maxC), maxC, C
```
But scoring via 2n maxC equals score if p sum1. evaluate_sequence will clip to 1000 etc; p<=1. Use own.

`conv_normalized`? 

`interpolate_p(p,new_n)`:
```
def interpolate_p(p,new_n):
    p=np.asarray(p,float); n=len(p)
    if n==new_n: return p/p.sum()
    s=p.sum(); if s<1e-12: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    old=np.linspace(0,1,n); new=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    p_int=np.interp(new,old,p/s)
    p_int=np.maximum(p_int,0); ss=p_int.sum(); return p_int/ss if ss>0 else ones
```
For n large, np.interp okay.

`softmax_p(theta,n)`:
```
def softmax_p(theta,n):
    theta=np.asarray(theta,float)
    theta=theta-np.max(theta)
    theta=np.clip(theta,-50,50)
    e=np.exp(theta)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.ones(n)/n
    return e/s
```
This returns p sum1. Need theta length n. If n maybe 0. 
`p_to_theta(p)`:
```
def p_to_theta(p):
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); mean=p.mean(); theta=np.log(p/mean); theta-=theta.mean(); return theta
```

`cross_corr(w,p)` as above.
```
def cross_corr(w,p):
    n=len(p); L=w.size
    size=next_pow2(L+n-1)
    wr=np.asarray(w,float64); pr=np.asarray(p[::-1],float64)
    W=np.fft.rfft(wr,size); P=np.fft.rfft(pr,size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(W*P,size)[:L+n-2]  # length L+n-1? Wait conv length L+n-1. slice n-1:2n-1 inclusive -> length n.
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check slice: conv length L+n-1 = (2n-1)+n-1=3n-2. segment n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n. In Python `conv[n-1:2*n-1]`? End exclusive 2n-1 gives indices n-1..2n-2 length n. Yes.

- cross = cross_corr(w,p)
- mean = float(np.dot(p,cross))
- grad = p*(cross-mean)
   # To avoid grad zero if symmetric, if norm grad tiny, add small random to theta? But m/v handle.
- Adam: t+=1
   m=...; v=...
   m_hat=m/(1-b1**t); v_hat=v/(1-b2**t)
   step=lr * m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+1e-8)
   # optionally scale step: if too large, clip: step = np.clip(step, -theta_bound? )?

   # soft clip theta: if max>30, scale down? Instead clip: theta=np.clip(theta,-35,35). But after mean, clipping may break recenter. Do: theta = np.clip(theta, -40,40); theta -= theta.mean().
- p=softmax_p(theta,n)

- if not finite: revert

    p_best copy; theta = p_to_theta(best_p); theta += rng.normal(0, noise_scale, n); theta-=mean; m,v=0; beta=max(beta*0.6,beta_start*0.5); reset_count++

Return best_p,best_H.

Fourier objective and search.

`fourier_objective(coeffs, A, N)`:
```
def fourier_objective(coeffs, A, N):
   f = 1.0 + A.dot(coeffs)
   f = np.clip(f,0.0,None)
   s=f.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12: return 1e6
   p=f/s
   C=fast_conv(p)
   return float(N * C.max())
```
If coefficients outside bounds produce negative, clipped. Clipping changes mean; normalization handles. But clipped zeros. 
Need `A` shape (N,D) and coeffs shape (D,). A.dot(coeffs) works.

Local peak balance code details:

```
def local_balance(p0, time_limit, seed=0, verbose=True):
    n=len(p0); p=norm; C=fast_conv(p); maxC=C.max(); best_p=p.copy(); bestC=maxC; currentC=maxC
    t0=time.monotonic(); rng; stall=0; n_accept=0; A_lags=min(30, max(10,n//1000?))
    frac_list = [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7]
    max_tries_per_round=150
    while time < time_limit and stall < 2000:
        # maybe recompute C if drift
        if n_accept and n_accept % 50 ==0:
            C=fast_conv(p); cur=C.max(); if cur < currentC - 1e-12: currentC=cur
            # if p sum not 1 renorm
        if currentC < bestC: update
        # top lags
        L=2*n-1
        A = max(8, min(40, n//500?))
        idx=np.argpartition(C, -A)[-A:]
        # pressure
        pressure=np.zeros(n)
        for k in idx:
            s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
            if s<=e: pressure[s:e+1] += p[k - np.arange(s,e+1)] # using array
        src_score = p*pressure
        low_pressure = np.argsort(pressure)[:100]  # bottom
        improved=False; tries=0
        for k in idx[:min(10,A)]:
            if improved: break
            s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k); idxk=np.arange(s,e+1)
            products = p[idxk]*p[k-idxk]
            top_pairs = np.argsort(products)[-3:]
            for pi in top_pairs:
                if tries>max_tries: break
                src_i=int(idxk[pi]); partner=k-src_i
                if partner<0 or partner>=n: continue
                if p[src_i]>=p[partner]: red=src_i; keep=partner else red=partner; keep=src_i
                if p[red]<=1e-12: continue
                # destination candidates:
                dests = []
                # global low pressure
                for d in low_pressure[:30]:
                    if d != red: dests.append(int(d))
                # specific low partner for this k:
                # Need list of dst where partner k-dst in [0,n-1] and p[k-dst] small
                # The valid dst range: max(0,k-n+1) to min(n-1,k) (same as idxk). Candidate dst in this range has partner k-dst. Also dst outside valid has partner invalid (C contribution zero for this k) and maybe safer. We can include outside too with low pressure.
                # For specific, sort part = p[k-dst] for valid dst; add lowest 20
                valid_dst = np.arange(s,e+1)
                part_vals = p[k - valid_dst]
                part_order = np.argsort(part_vals)[:20]
                for d in valid_dst[part_order]:
                    d=int(d)
                    if d != red and d not in dests: dests.append(d)
                # Also global top? maybe.
                # Try fracs
                for fract in [0.05,0.02,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7]:
                    delta = fract*p[red]
                    if delta<=0: continue
                    Ct=C.copy()
                    # linear updates
                    Ct[src_i:src_i+n] -= 2.0*delta*p[0:n]
                    Ct[dst:dst+n] += 2.0*delta*p[0:n]
                    # quadratic
                    q1=2*src_i
                    if 0<=q1<L: Ct[q1]+=delta*delta
                    q2=2*dst
                    if 0<=q2<L: Ct[q2]+=delta*delta
                    q3=src_i+dst
                    if 0<=q3<L: Ct[q3]-=2.0*delta*delta
                    newmax = Ct.max()
                    tries +=1
                    if newmax < currentC - 1e-15*currentC:
                        p[red]-=delta; p[dst]+=delta
                        # normalize if needed tiny
                        ssum=p.sum()
                        if abs(ssum-1)>1e-9: p/=ssum
                        C=Ct; currentC=newmax
                        n_accept+=1
                        improved=True
                        break
                if improved: break
            if improved: break
        if not improved:
            # random moves
            for _ in range(20):
                red=int(rng.integers(n)); dst=int(rng.integers(n)); if red==dst or p[red]<=0: continue
                delta=rng.uniform(0.005,0.05)*p[red]
                Ct=C.copy(); update; newmax=Ct.max(); tries...
                if newmax < currentC - ...: accept
                else if improved break
            if not improved: stall+=1
        else: stall=0
        if stall > 200:
            # revert to best and recompute
            p=best_p.copy(); C=fast_conv(p); currentC=bestC; stall=0
            # add small random noise to p? maybe not
        # periodic recompute C to clean
        if n_accept % 100 ==0:
            C=fast_conv(p); currentC=C.max()
            if currentC < bestC: update
        # If no improvement for long and best not current, maybe do gradient opt? External.
        if verbose and stall%500==0: print
    return best_p,bestC
```

Need ensure no `d not in dests` for potentially large list O(300) okay. Could use set but no lambda. Use Python list.

Quadratic update: Need use old p slices. But if dst and src slices overlap, the linear updates are commutative and use old p. That's correct. However if we update Ct[src:src+n] then Ct[dst:dst+n], both use p unaffected. Good.

When adding delta to Ct[2dst] and decrementing cross: if 2dst same as src+dst or 2src, operations add. Use separate if with same index okay. Order doesn't matter. Example src=dst? We skip.

But linear update for source decrease: C[src:src+n] -= 2δ p[:n]. For k in src..src+n-1, term -2δ p[k-src]. Then quadratic at 2src +=δ². For k=2src, -2δ p[src]+δ². Good. For dst increase: +2δ p[:n]. For k=2dst, +2δ p[dst]+δ² from new (qdst+δ)²: old qdst², new qdst²+2δ qdst+δ². Good. Cross term -2δ² at src+dst. If src+dst falls in both slices, linear already includes contributions involving old values; quadratic cross accounts product of changes. Works.

Potential issue: If p[red] - delta > - tiny; ensure nonneg. Use delta=min(frac*p[red], p[red]*0.9). Good.

Max tries: C.copy each trial heavy. Try low_pressure list might include red. filtered.

`pressure` addition: `k - np.arange(s,e+1)` creates array. For performance, `pressure[s:e+1] += p[k-e:k-s+1][::-1]` maybe. Since k-idx from k-s down to k-e; we can use slice reversed: `p[k-e:k-s+1][::-1]`. Simpler use array.

def fourier_search(time_limit, seed=7):
   if time_limit<30: return []
   D=8; N=4096; pop=20
   A = np.empty((N,D), dtype=np.float64)
   x = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
   for d in range(D): A[:,d] = np.cos((2*d+1)*math.pi*x)
   rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
   # initialize pop
   pop_coeffs=[]
   pop_coeffs.append(np.zeros(D))
   # single positive modes for first 3
   for d in range(min(3,D)):
      c=np.zeros(D); c[d]=0.8; pop_coeffs.append(c)
   # single negative? maybe
   for d in range(min(2,D)):
      c=np.zeros(D); c[d]=-0.8; pop_coeffs.append(c)
   # random
   while len(pop_coeffs)<pop:
      pop_coeffs.append(rng.uniform(-0.6,0.6,D))
   # evaluate function
   best_list=[]  # top 3 tuples (H, coeffs)
   def? no closures. We'll write inner loop top-level? Can't nest. Need evaluate helper:
```

`coeff_to_p`:
```
def coeff_to_p(c,A,N):
    f=1.0 + A.dot(c)
    f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.ones(N)/N
    return f/s
```

```
def score_p(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(p)
    if n==0: return (float('inf'), float('inf'), np.array([]))
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-15: return ...
    p=p/s
    C=fast_conv(p)
    C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
    maxC=float(C.max()) if C.size else 0.0
    score=2.0*float(n)*maxC
    return score, maxC, C
```
Note score=2n maxC when sum1. Internal H= n maxC = score/2.

`cross_corr`:
```
def cross_corr(w,p):
    n=len(p)
    L=len(w)
    size=_next_pow2(L+n-1)
    pr=np.asarray(p[::-1], dtype=np.float64)
    wr=np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    Wr=np.fft.rfft(wr,size)
    Pr=np.fft.rfft(pr,size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(Wr*Pr,size)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Ensure length: conv size `size`, but linear length maybe less; slicing okay if size >. It may include circular tail? Since size >= full length, first full_len correct. Good.

```
def fourier_search(time_limit, seed=17):
   res=[]
   if time_limit < 45: return res
   D=8; N=4096
   x=np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64)/float(N)
   A=np.empty((N,D), dtype=np.float64)
   for d in range(D): A[:,d]=np.cos((2*d+1)*math.pi*x)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   pop=[]
   # fill
   c=np.zeros(D); pop.append(c)
   for d in range(min(4,D)):
      c=np.zeros(D); c[d]=0.8; pop.append(c.copy())
      c=np.zeros(D); c[d]=-0.8; pop.append(c.copy())
   while len(pop)<24:
      pop.append(rng.uniform(-0.6,0.6,D))
   top=[]
   # evaluate initial
   for c in pop:
      H=fourier_eval(c,A,N)
      update_top(top,H,c,3)
   end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
   sigma=0.35
   stale=0
   gen=0
   while time.monotonic() < end - 5:
      scores=[]
      for c in pop: scores.append(fourier_eval(c,A,N))
      order=np.argsort(scores)
      keep=8
      newpop=[]
      for i in range(min(keep,len(order))): newpop.append(pop[i].copy())
      # generate
      while len(newpop)<len(pop):
         i1=int(order[rng.integers(min(keep+6,len(order)))])
         i2=int(order[rng.integers(min(keep+6,len(order)))])
         child=0.5*(pop[i1]+pop[i2]) + rng.normal(0.0,sigma,D)
         child=np.clip(child,-1.0,1.0)
         newpop.append(child)
      pop=newpop
      before=top[0][0] if top else inf
      for c in pop:
         H=fourier_eval(c,A,N)
         update_top(top,H,c,3)
      if top and top[0][0] < before:
         stale=0; sigma=min(0.6,sigma*1.05)
      else:
         stale+=1; if stale>3: sigma*=0.8; stale=0
      gen+=1
      if now > end - 15: break
   out=[]
   for H,c in top:
      p=coeff_to_p(c,A,N)
      out.append((p, float(H)))
   return out
```

- Keep `pop` list and `scores` list evaluated. Each gen: generate newpop, evaluate newpop, combine (pop+newpop) and select best 24 by scores, update top. This doubles memory but fine. Avoid re-evaluating old.

def local_balance(p0, time_limit, seed=1, verbose=False):
   t0=...; end=t0+time_limit
   p=np.maximum(np.asarray(p0,float),0); s=p.sum(); if s<... return p0,inf
   p=p/s; n=len(p); C=fast_conv(p); C=np.maximum(C,0); curC=C.max(); best_p=p.copy(); bestC=curC
   rng=...
   stall=0; accepts=0; round=0
   maxC_tol factor=1e-12
   A_count = max(8, min(30, n//400))
   max_tries_round = max(30, min(200, 3000000//n))
   fracs = [0.05,0.02,0.1,0.2,0.4]
   while time.monotonic()<end:
      if curC < bestC: bestC=curC; best_p=p.copy()
      if not np.isfinite(bestC): break
      # top lags
      L=2*n-1
      idx = np.argpartition(C, -A_count)[-A_count:] if A_count<L else np.arange(L)
      # pressure
      pressure=np.zeros(n)
      for k in idx:
          k=int(k); s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
          if s<=e:
             pressure[s:e+1] += p[k-e:k-s+1][::-1]  # check slice: k-e ... k-s? For s=0,e=2,k=5: k-idx = [5,4,3], slice k-e=3 to k-s=5 gives [3,4,5], reversed [5,4,3] correct.
      low_count = min(80,n-1)
      low_pressure = np.argsort(pressure)[:low_count] # ascending
      improved=False; tries=0
       min_lags= min(8,A_count)
      for k in idx[:min_lags]:
          if improved: break
          k=int(k); s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
          if s>e: continue
          idxk=np.arange(s,e+1)
          part=p[k-e:k-s+1][::-1]
          products=p[idxk]*part
          if products.max() <=0: continue
          if len(products)>3:
             top_pairs=np.argpartition(products, -3)[-3:]
          else:
             top_pairs=np.arange(len(products))
          for pi in top_pairs:
             if tries >= max_tries_round: break
             src=int(idxk[pi]); partner=k-src; ppart=part[pi]
             if partner<0 or partner>=n: continue
             if p[src] >= p[partner]: red=src; keep=partner
             else: red=partner; keep=src
             pr=p[red]
             if pr <= 1e-13: continue
             # build dest list
             dests=[]
             for dd in low_pressure[:30]:
                dd=int(dd)
                if dd != red:
                   dests.append(dd)
             # specific low partner
             valid_count=e-s+1
             if valid_count>0:
                part_vals = p[ (k - np.arange(s,e+1)) ]
                # We can use part array (p partners for idxk) for invalid? For any dst, partner = k-dst; valid dst range same. We already have part_vals for dst=idxk. Use part.
                take = min(20, valid_count)
                part_order=np.argpartition(part, take-1)[:take] if take<valid_count else np.arange(valid_count)
                for oo in part_order:
                    dd=int(idxk[oo])
                    if dd != red and dd not in dests: dests.append(dd)
             # limit dest list 25
             for dst in dests:
                if len(dests processed? we can while tries
                if dst==red: continue
                for frac in fracs:
                   if tries>=max_tries: break
                   delta=frac*pr
                   if delta<=1e-18: continue
                   # quick active peak reduction
                   add_idx=dst? partner for dst: val=k-dst if valid else 0
                   add = p[k-dst] if 0<=k-dst<n else 0.0
                   # But if red=partner? If red chosen as larger of pair, partner is original other. If we reduce partner instead? We choose red one of pair. The partner for active lag after moving from red is still p[k-red] (old). If red=src? yes.
                   # quick using oldC[k]
                   base=C[k]
                   ck = base - 2.0*delta*ppart + 2.0*delta*add
                   if k == 2*dst: ck += delta*delta
                   if k == 2*red: ck += delta*delta
                   if k == red+dst: ck -= 2.0*delta*delta
                   if ck >= base - 1e-16*max(1.0,abs(base)): continue
                   Ct=C.copy()
                   Ct[red:red+n] -= 2.0*delta*p
                   Ct[dst:dst+n] += 2.0*delta*p
                   q1=2*red
                   if 0 <= q1 < L: Ct[q1] += delta*delta
                   q2=2*dst
                   if 0 <= q2 < L: Ct[q2] += delta*delta
                   q3=red+dst
                   if 0 <= q3 < L: Ct[q3] -= 2.0*delta*delta
                   newCmax=float(Ct.max())
                   tries += 1
                   if newCmax < curC - max(1e-15, 1e-12*curC):
                       p[red] -= delta; p[dst] += delta
                       ssum=p.sum()
                       if abs(ssum-1.0) > 1e-8:
                           p = p/ssum
                           C = fast_conv(p); C = np.maximum(C,0.0); curC = float(C.max())
                       else:
                           C = Ct; curC = newCmax
                       accepts += 1
                       improved = True
                       if curC < bestC:
                           bestC=curC; best_p=p.copy()
                       break
                if improved: break
             if improved: break
          if improved: break
      if not improved:
         # random moves
         for _ in range(15):
            if tries >= max_tries_round: break
            red=int(rng.integers(n)); dst=int(rng.integers(n))
            if red==dst or p[red]<=1e-13: continue
            delta=rng.uniform(0.005,0.05)*p[red]
            Ct=C.copy()
            Ct[red:red+n] -= 2.0*delta*p
            Ct[dst:dst+n] += 2.0*delta*p
            q1=2*red; q2=2*dst; q3=red+dst ...
            newCmax=float(Ct.max()); tries+=1
            if newCmax < curC - max(1e-15,1e-12*curC): accept...
            if improved: break
         if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 300:
                # perturb best
                p = best_p.copy()
                noise_scale = 0.002 * max(1.0, 1.0/np.sqrt(n)? actually relative p ~1/n? Need absolute noise ~1/n? p_avg=1/n. Use noise_scale=1e-4/sqrt(n)? For n=10k, 3e-6. Maybe too small. Use 1e-5.
                p = np.maximum(p + rng.normal(0, 1e-5, n), 0.0)
                ssum=p.sum(); p/=ssum if ssum>0
                C=fast_conv(p); C=np.maximum(C,0); curC=C.max()
                if curC < bestC: update
                stall=0
      else:
         stall = 0
         if accepts % 50 == 0:
             C=fast_conv(p); C=np.maximum(C,0); curC=C.max()
             if curC < bestC: update
      round +=1
      if verbose and round % 100 ==0: print
   return best_p, bestC
```

Now softmax_opt code details. Need avoid if no top. Use p0 normalized.

```
def softmax_opt(p0, time_limit, seed=1, beta_start=5.0, beta_end=800.0, top_k_frac=0.02, lr0=0.06, verbose=False):
   if time_limit <=0: return p0 copy, inf
   p=norm; n=len(p); C=fast_conv(p); H=C*n; bestH=max; best_p=p.copy()
   theta=p_to_theta(p)
   m=zeros; v=zeros; t=0; beta=beta_start; stall=0; resetcnt=0
   L=2n-1; top_k=max(20, min(800,int(n*top_k_frac)))
   w=zeros(L); old_idx=None
   b1=0.9; b2=0.999
   end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
   while time.monotonic()<end:
      if t % 500 ==0:
         beta = min(beta*1.6, beta_end)
      # weights
      if top_k >= L:
         idx = np.arange(L)
      else:
         if old_idx is not None:
             w[old_idx]=0.0
         idx = np.argpartition(H, -top_k)[-top_k:]
      htop=H[idx]; gmax=float(htop.max()) if htop.size else 0.0
      if not np.isfinite(gmax): H = np.maximum(H,0); gmax=H.max()
      shift=beta*(htop-gmax); shift=np.clip(shift, -50.0, 0.0)
      vals=np.exp(shift); tot=vals.sum()
      if tot<=0 or not finite: vals=np.ones(htop.size)/size
      else: vals=vals/tot
      w[idx]=vals
      if top_k < L: old_idx=idx
      cross=cross_corr(w,p)
      mean_cross=float(np.dot(p,cross))
      grad=p*(cross-mean_cross)
      t +=1
      m=b1*m+(1-b1)*grad; v=b2*v+(1-b2)*grad*grad
      mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
      lr = lr0
      # if current H max > best*1.02, half lr
      cur_before = float(H.max())
      if cur_before > bestH*1.05: lr*=0.5
      theta = theta - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
      theta -= theta.mean()
      theta = np.clip(theta, -20.0, 20.0)
      theta -= theta.mean()
      p = softmax_p(theta,n)
      C=fast_conv(p); C=np.maximum(C,0.0); H=C*float(n); cur=float(H.max())
      if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < bestH:
         bestH=cur; best_p=p.copy(); stall=0
      else:
         stall += 1
      if stall > 1500 or (t>1000 and cur > bestH*1.05):
         resetcnt +=1
         p_best=best_p
         theta=p_to_theta(p_best)
         # noise scale relative to theta distribution
         noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
         theta += noise
         theta -= theta.mean(); theta=np.clip(theta,-20,20); theta-=theta.mean()
         m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; b1**t? Adam bias correction with t reset but v=zeros.
         beta=max(beta*0.5, beta_start*0.5)
         stall=0
   return best_p, bestH
```

```
def p_to_theta(p):
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-18)
   mean=p.mean()
   if mean<=0: return zeros
   theta=np.log(p/mean)
   theta-=theta.mean()
   return theta
```

def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total=min(float(budget_s)-90.0, 1710.0)
    if total <= 0: total=30.0
    end=t0+total
    best_seq=None; best_score=float('inf')
    parent_n=0
    # load parent
    try:
       raw=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       raw=np.clip(raw,0.0,1000.0)
       raw=raw[~np.isnan(raw)]? can't with boolean? `raw = raw[np.isfinite(raw)]`
       if raw.size>0:
          s=raw.sum()
          if s>1e-8:
             p=raw/s; sc=score_p(p)[0]; best_seq=p; best_score=sc; parent_n=len(p); print
    except Exception:
       pass
    if best_seq is None:
       n_def=6700
       p=np.ones(n_def)/n_def
       best_seq=p; best_score=score_p(p)[0]; parent_n=n_def; print
    # candidates list
    cand_ps=[best_seq]; cand_scores=[best_score]
    # fourier search
    now=time.monotonic(); rem=end-now
    t_fourier = min(180.0, max(0.0, rem*0.12))
    if rem > 400 and t_fourier > 30:
        fourier = fourier_search(t_fourier, seed=17)
        base_n = max(1000, len(best_seq))
        for pN, H in fourier:
            lengths = [base_n]
            for L in [base_n+2000, base_n+5000, 12000, 16000]:
                if L not in lengths and 3000 <= L <= 20000: lengths.append(L)
            # also if parent len not 12000 etc
            for L in lengths:
                pL=interpolate_p(pN,L)
                sc,_,_=score_p(pL)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    cand_ps.append(pL); cand_scores.append(sc)
    # sort candidates: take top 4 distinct-ish
    if len(cand_ps)>4:
       order=np.argsort(cand_scores)
       new_ps=[]; new_scores=[]
       for idx in order:
           if len(new_ps)>=4: break
           # To ensure diversity, maybe if p allclose to previous skip? skip.
           new_ps.append(cand_ps[idx]); new_scores.append(cand_scores[idx])
       cand_ps=new_ps; cand_scores=new_scores
    # main candidate = best (cand_ps[0])
    main_p=cand_ps[0]
    # local initial on main
    rem=end-now

    if t_loc>30:
       p_out,H=local_balance(main_p, t_loc, seed=11, verbose=False)
       best_seq,best_score=try_update_best(best_seq,best_score,p_out,"loc0")
    # softmax main
    rem=end-now
    t_sm = min(500.0, max(0.0, rem*0.30))
    if t_sm>60:
       p_out,H=softmax_opt(main_p, t_sm, seed=21, beta_start=5,beta_end=900, top_k_frac=0.02, lr0=0.06)
       best_seq,best_score=try_update_best(best_seq,best_score,p_out,"soft1")
    # local after softmax

    t_loc2 = min(300.0, max(0.0, rem*0.22))
    if t_loc2>40:
       p_out,H=local_balance(best_seq, t_loc2, seed=22, verbose=False)
       best_seq,best_score=try_update_best(best_seq,best_score,p_out,"loc1")
    # second candidate if exists and different score? use cand_ps[1] if available and not same object?
    if len(cand_ps) > 1:
       rem=end-now
       second=cand_ps[1]
       # If second has length very different? okay
       t_sm2 = min(300.0, max(0.0, rem*0.18))
       if t_sm2>60:
          # maybe if second score > best_score*1.02, use interpolated to best length?
          p_second=second
          p_out,H=softmax_opt(p_second, t_sm2, seed=31, beta_start=10,beta_end=700, top_k_frac=0.02, lr0=0.05)
          best_seq,best_score=try_update_best(...)
       rem=end-now
       t_loc3 = min(180.0, max(0, rem*0.10))
       if t_loc3>30:
          p_out,H=local_balance(p_out if defined else best_seq,...)
    # multi-length from current best
    rem=end-now
    n_best=len(best_seq)
    multi=[]
    for L in [n_best+2000, n_best+5000, n_best+9000, max(3000,n_best-2000)]:
       if 3000 <= L <= 20000 and L not in multi: multi.append(L)
    for i,L in enumerate(multi):
       rem=end-now
       if rem<120: break
       p_int=interpolate_p(best_seq,L)
       sc,_,_=score_p(p_int)
       if sc<best_score: best_seq=p_int; best_score=sc; print direct
       # short softmax
       t_ms = min(90.0, rem*0.25)
       if t_ms>20:
          p_out,H=softmax_opt(p_int, t_ms, seed=41+i, beta_start=15,beta_end=600, top_k_frac=0.02, lr0=0.04)
          sc2=score_p(p_out)[0]
          if sc2<best_score: update
       rem=end-now
       t_ml = min(70.0, rem*0.20)
       if t_ml>15:
          p_out2,H=local_balance(best_seq, t_ml, seed=51+i)
          update
    # final local
    rem=end-now
    t_final = min(300.0, rem*0.35)
    if t_final>30:
       p_out,H=local_balance(best_seq, t_final, seed=99)
       update
    # final softmax high beta tiny
    rem=end-now
    t_final2 = min(100.0, rem*0.3)
    if t_final2>20:
       p_out,H=softmax_opt(best_seq, t_final2, seed=888, beta_start=50,beta_end=1200, top_k_frac=0.01, lr0=0.03)
       update
    # ensure valid
    best_seq=np.asarray(best_seq,float)
    best_seq=np.maximum(best_seq,0.0)
    best_seq=np.nan_to_num(best_seq, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    s=best_seq.sum()
    if s<1e-8: fallback uniform 6700
    best_seq=best_seq/s
    best_seq=np.clip(best_seq,0.0,1000.0)
    # optional final direct score print using score_p not evaluate_sequence
    sc,_,_=score_p(best_seq)
    print final
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]

```python
"""
Search for low-maximum self-convolution step functions.

The objective, for nonnegative a with positive sum S, is
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/S^2.
Normalize by S so we optimize p with sum 1 and minimize H = n*max(conv(p,p)),
score = 2H.

Main tools:
- Global exploration by finite odd-cosine basis profiles (differentiable in the
  sense that it can be hill-climbed globally with a small population).
- Softmax/natural-gradient optimization of the log-sum-exp smoothed max
  (using FFT-based gradient as a cross-correlation with active-peak weights).
- Very targeted O(n) "peak surgery" in the convolution table, updating the
  convolution exactly for candidate mass moves and accepting only decreases.
- Multi-length interpolation/polishing at the end.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def score_p(p):
    """Return (score, max_convolution, convolution) for a sum-normalized p."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return float("inf"), float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s
    c = fast_conv(p)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mc), mc, c


def norm_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones_like(p, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, len(p))
    return p / s


def interpolate_p(p, new_n):
    p = norm_p(p)
    old_n = len(p)
    if new_n == old_n:
        return p.copy()
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(new_x, old_x, p)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    return out / s


def p_to_theta(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-18)
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    mean = float(p.mean())
    if mean <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    th = np.log(p / mean)
    th -= float(th.mean())
    return th


def softmax_p(theta, n):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    theta = theta - float(theta.max())
    theta = np.clip(theta, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(theta)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return e / s


def coeff_to_p(c, A, N):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    f = 1.0 + A.dot(c)
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64) / N
    return f / s


def fourier_eval(c, A, N):
    p = coeff_to_p(c, A, N)
    cv = fast_conv(p)
    if cv.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(N * float(np.maximum(cv, 0.0).max()))


def update_top(top_list, H, c, max_top=3):
    top_list.append((float(H), np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    m = len(top_list)
    for i in range(m - 1):
        if top_list[i][0] > top_list[i + 1][0]:
            tmp = top_list[i]
            top_list[i] = top_list[i + 1]
            top_list[i + 1] = tmp
    while len(top_list) > max_top:
        top_list.pop()


def fourier_search(time_limit, seed=17):
    if time_limit < 60:
        return []
    D = 8
    N = 4096
    x = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    A = np.empty((N, D), dtype=np.float64)
    for d in range(D):
        A[:, d] = np.cos((2 * d + 1) * math.pi * x)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    pop = []

    c0 = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    pop.append(c0)
    for d in range(min(4, D)):
        c = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        c[d] = 0.75
        pop.append(c)
        c = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        c[d] = -0.75
        pop.append(c)
    while len(pop) < 24:
        pop.append(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6, D).astype(np.float64))

    top = []
    scores = []
    for c in pop:
        h = fourier_eval(c, A, N)
        scores.append(h)
        update_top(top, h, c, 3)

    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, time_limit)
    sigma = 0.35
    stale = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end - 10.0:
        order = np.argsort(scores)
        keep = 8
        new_pop = []

        # Keep globally best coefficients.
        for _, c in top[:3]:
            new_pop.append(c.copy())

        # Keep a few current best.
        for i in range(min(max(0, keep - len(new_pop)), len(order))):
            new_pop.append(pop[order[i]].copy())

        # Fill population.
        while len(new_pop) < len(pop):
            hi = min(keep + 6, len(order))
            i1 = int(order[rng.integers(hi)])
            i2 = int(order[rng.integers(hi)])
            child = 0.5 * (pop[i1] + pop[i2]) + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, D)
            child = np.clip(child, -1.0, 1.0)
            new_pop.append(child.astype(np.float64))

        before = top[0][0] if top else float("inf")
        scores = []
        for c in new_pop:
            h = fourier_eval(c, A, N)
            scores.append(h)
            update_top(top, h, c, 3)
        pop = new_pop

        if top and top[0][0] < before:
            stale = 0
            sigma = min(0.55, sigma * 1.04)
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale >= 3:
                sigma = max(0.05, sigma * 0.82)
                stale = 0

    out = []
    for h, c in top:
        p = coeff_to_p(c, A, N)
        out.append((p, float(h)))
    return out


def try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p, label):
    if p is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return best_seq, best_score
    arr = arr / s
    sc, _, _ = score_p(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        print("  NEW BEST [" + str(label) + "]: " + repr(sc) +
              " n=" + str(len(arr)) + " t=" +
              str(round(time.monotonic(), 1)) + "s")
        return arr, sc
    return best_seq, best_score


def softmax_opt(p0, time_limit, seed=1, beta_start=5.0, beta_end=900.0,
                top_k_frac=0.02, lr0=0.06, verbose=False):
    """Natural-gradient/Adam optimizer in softmax coordinates."""
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        p = norm_p(p0)
        if len(p) == 0:
            p = np.ones(1000, dtype=np.float64) / 1000
        return p, float("inf")

    p = norm_p(p0)
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        p = np.ones(1000, dtype=np.float64) / 1000
        n = len(p)

    size = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    cv = fast_conv(p)
    cv = np.maximum(cv, 0.0)
    H = cv * float(n)
    bestH = float(H.max())
    best_p = p.copy()

    theta = p_to_theta(p)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    top_k = int(round(n * top_k_frac))
    top_k = max(20, min(800, top_k))
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    old_idx = None

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    t = 0
    beta = float(beta_start)
    stall = 0
    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        if t > 0 and t % 450 == 0:
            beta = min(beta * 1.55, float(beta_end))

        if old_idx is not None:
            w[old_idx] = 0.0

        if top_k >= L:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(H, -top_k)[-top_k:]

        htop = H[idx]
        if htop.size:
            gmax = float(htop.max())
        else:
            gmax = 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(gmax):
            gmax = 0.0

        shift = beta * (htop - gmax)
        shift = np.clip(shift, -50.0, 0.0)
        vals = np.exp(shift)
        tot = float(vals.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(tot) or tot <= 0.0:
            vals = np.ones(htop.size, dtype=np.float64)
            tot = float(vals.sum()) if vals.size else 1.0
        if tot > 0.0:
            vals = vals / tot
        w[idx] = vals
        if top_k < L:
            old_idx = idx
        else:
            old_idx = None

        # Autocorrelation and cross-correlation share one FFT of p.
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
        cv = np.fft.irfft(P * P, size)[:L]
        cv = np.maximum(cv, 0.0)
        H = cv * float(n)
        cur_before = float(H.max())

        W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
        cross = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(P), size)[:n]
        cross = np.asarray(cross, dtype=np.float64)

        mean_cross = float(np.dot(p, cross))
        grad = p * (cross - mean_cross)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            theta = p_to_theta(best_p)
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            stall = 0
            continue

        t += 1
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * grad
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (grad * grad)

        mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        eta = float(lr0)
        if cur_before > bestH * 1.06:
            eta *= 0.55

        step = eta * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(step)):
            step = np.zeros_like(step)

        theta = theta - step
        theta = np.clip(theta, -20.0, 20.0)
        theta -= float(theta.mean())

        p = softmax_p(theta, n)
        cv = fast_conv(p)
        cv = np.maximum(cv, 0.0)
        H = cv * float(n)
        cur = float(H.max())

        if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < bestH:
            bestH = cur
            best_p = p.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall > 1600 or (t > 800 and cur > bestH * 1.06):
            theta = p_to_theta(best_p)
            theta = theta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -20.0, 20.0)
            theta -= float(theta.mean())
            m.fill(0.0)
            v.fill(0.0)
            t = 0
            beta = max(float(beta_start) * 0.5, beta * 0.55)
            stall = 0

        if verbose and t % 2000 == 0:
            print("    softmax t=" + str(t) +
                  " best_score=" + repr(2.0 * bestH) +
                  " beta=" + str(round(beta, 1)))

    return best_p, bestH


def local_balance(p0, time_limit, seed=1, verbose=False):
    """Exact-convolution local peak surgery with O(n) move updates."""
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        p = norm_p(p0)
        cv = fast_conv(p)
        return p, (0.0 if cv.size == 0 else float(cv.max()))

    p = norm_p(p0)
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        p = np.ones(1000, dtype=np.float64) / 1000
        n = len(p)

    cv = fast_conv(p)
    cv = np.maximum(cv, 0.0)
    curC = float(cv.max())
    bestC = curC
    best_p = p.copy()

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    A_count = max(8, min(32, n // 350))
    A_count = min(A_count, 2 * n - 1)
    max_tries_round = max(30, min(180, 3000000 // max(1, n)))
    fracs = (0.05, 0.02, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    accept_count = 0
    stall = 0
    rnd = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        if curC < bestC:
            bestC = curC
            best_p = p.copy()

        if A_count >= L:
            top_lags = np.arange(L)
        else:
            top_lags = np.argpartition(cv, -A_count)[-A_count:]

        pressure = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in top_lags:
            k = int(kk)
            s = max(0, k - n + 1)
            e = min(n - 1, k)
            if s <= e:
                pressure[s:e + 1] += p[k - e:k - s + 1][::-1]

        low_count = min(80, n)
        low_pressure = np.argsort(pressure)[:low_count] if low_count > 0 else np.array([0], dtype=int)

        improved = False
        tries = 0
        tol_abs = max(1e-15, 1e-12 * float(curC))

        min_lags = min(10, len(top_lags))
        for kk in top_lags[:min_lags]:
            if improved:
                break
            k = int(kk)
            s = max(0, k - n + 1)
            e = min(n - 1, k)
            if s > e:
                continue
            idxk = np.arange(s, e + 1)
            part = p[k - e:k - s + 1][::-1]
            products = p[idxk] * part
            if products.size == 0 or products.max() <= 0.0:
                continue

            if products.size > 3:
                pair_idx = np.argpartition(products, -3)[-3:]
            else:
                pair_idx = np.arange(products.size)

            for pi in pair_idx:
                if tries >= max_tries_round:
                    break
                src = int(idxk[pi])
                partner = k - src
                if partner < 0 or partner >= n:
                    continue
                if p[src] >= p[partner]:
                    red = src
                    ppart = p[partner]
                else:
                    red = partner
                    ppart = p[src]

                if p[red] <= 1e-13:
                    continue

                dests = []
                for dd in low_pressure[:25]:
                    dd = int(dd)
                    if dd != red and dd not in dests:
                        dests.append(dd)

                valid_count = e - s + 1
                if valid_count > 0:
                    take = min(18, valid_count)
                    if take < valid_count:
                        part_order = np.argpartition(part, take - 1)[:take]
                    else:
                        part_order = np.arange(valid_count)
                    for oo in part_order:
                        dd = int(idxk[oo])
                        if dd != red and dd not in dests:
                            dests.append(dd)

                for dst in dests:
                    dst = int(dst)
                    if dst == red:
                        continue
                    if tries >= max_tries_round:
                        break

                    for frac in fracs:
                        if tries >= max_tries_round:
                            break
                        delta = frac * p[red]
                        if delta <= 1e-18:
                            continue

                        # Quick check on the target peak.
                        add_idx = k - dst
                        add = p[add_idx] if 0 <= add_idx < n else 0.0
                        base = cv[k]
                        ck = base - 2.0 * delta * ppart + 2.0 * delta * add
                        if k == 2 * dst:
                            ck += delta * delta
                        if k == 2 * red:
                            ck += delta * delta
                        if k == red + dst:
                            ck -= 2.0 * delta * delta
                        if ck >= base - 1e-15:
                            continue

                        ct = cv.copy()
                        ct[red:red + n] -= 2.0 * delta * p
                        ct[dst:dst + n] += 2.0 * delta * p

                        q1 = 2 * red
                        if 0 <= q1 < L:
                            ct[q1] += delta * delta
                        q2 = 2 * dst
                        if 0 <= q2 < L:
                            ct[q2] += delta * delta
                        q3 = red + dst
                        if 0 <= q3 < L:
                            ct[q3] -= 2.0 * delta * delta

                        newC = float(np.maximum(ct, 0.0).max())
                        tries += 1

                        if newC < curC - tol_abs:
                            p[red] -= delta
                            p[dst] += delta
                            ss = float(p.sum())
                            if abs(ss - 1.0) > 1e-8:
                                p = p / ss
                                cv = fast_conv(p)
                                cv = np.maximum(cv, 0.0)
                                curC = float(cv.max())
                            else:
                                cv = ct
                                curC = newC
                            accept_count += 1
                            improved = True
                            if curC < bestC:
                                bestC = curC
                                best_p = p.copy()
                            break
                    if improved:
                        break
                if improved:
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        # Generic pressure-based moves if targeted peak moves did not fire.
        if not improved and tries < max_tries_round:
            dep = p * pressure
            if dep.size > 12:
                src_candidates = np.argpartition(dep, -12)[-12:]
            else:
                src_candidates = np.arange(dep.size)

            for srcc in src_candidates[:10]:
                src = int(srcc)
                if p[src] <= 1e-13:
                    continue
                for dstc in low_pressure[:12]:
                    dst = int(dstc)
                    if dst == src:
                        continue
                    if tries >= max_tries_round:
                        break
                    for frac in (0.05, 0.10):
                        if tries >= max_tries_round:
                            break
                        delta = frac * p[src]
                        if delta <= 1e-18:
                            continue
                        ct = cv.copy()
                        ct[src:src + n] -= 2.0 * delta * p
                        ct[dst:dst + n] += 2.0 * delta * p

                        q1 = 2 * src
                        if 0 <= q1 < L:
                            ct[q1] += delta * delta
                        q2 = 2 * dst
                        if 0 <= q2 < L:
                            ct[q2] += delta * delta
                        q3 = src + dst
                        if 0 <= q3 < L:
                            ct[q3] -= 2.0 * delta * delta

                        newC = float(np.maximum(ct, 0.0).max())
                        tries += 1
                        if newC < curC - tol_abs:
                            p[src] -= delta
                            p[dst] += delta
                            ss = float(p.sum())
                            if abs(ss - 1.0) > 1e-8:
                                p = p / ss
                                cv = fast_conv(p)
                                cv = np.maximum(cv, 0.0)
                                curC = float(cv.max())
                            else:
                                cv = ct
                                curC = newC
                            accept_count += 1
                            improved = True
                            break
                    if improved:
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

        # Random exploration.
        if not improved:
            for _ in range(12):
                if tries >= max_tries_round:
                    break
                src = int(rng.integers(n))
                dst = int(rng.integers(n))
                if src == dst or p[src] <= 1e-13:
                    continue
                delta = rng.uniform(0.005, 0.045) * p[src]
                ct = cv.copy()
                ct[src:src + n] -= 2.0 * delta * p
                ct[dst:dst + n] += 2.0 * delta * p

                q1 = 2 * src
                if 0 <= q1 < L:
                    ct[q1] += delta * delta
                q2 = 2 * dst
                if 0 <= q2 < L:
                    ct[q2] += delta * delta
                q3 = src + dst
                if 0 <= q3 < L:
                    ct[q3] -= 2.0 * delta * delta

                newC = float(np.maximum(ct, 0.0).max())
                tries += 1
                if newC < curC - tol_abs:
                    p[src] -= delta
                    p[dst] += delta
                    ss = float(p.sum())
                    if abs(ss - 1.0) > 1e-8:
                        p = p / ss
                        cv = fast_conv(p)
                        cv = np.maximum(cv, 0.0)
                        curC = float(cv.max())
                    else:
                        cv = ct
                        curC = newC
                    accept_count += 1
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            if accept_count % 40 == 0:
                p = norm_p(p)
                cv = fast_conv(p)
                cv = np.maximum(cv, 0.0)
                curC = float(cv.max())
                if curC < bestC:
                    bestC = curC
                    best_p = p.copy()
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 400:
                noise_scale = 0.05 / float(n)
                pert = np.maximum(best_p + rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n), 0.0)
                ssum = float(pert.sum())
                if ssum > 0.0:
                    p = pert / ssum
                    cv = fast_conv(p)
                    cv = np.maximum(cv, 0.0)
                    curC = float(cv.max())
                    if curC < bestC:
                        bestC = curC
                        best_p = p.copy()
                stall = 0

        rnd += 1
        if verbose and rnd % 150 == 0:
            print("    local t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - (end - time_limit), 1)) +
                  "s best_score=" + repr(2.0 * bestC * n))

    return best_p, bestC


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = min(max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 90.0), 1710.0)
    if total <= 0.0:
        total = 30.0
    end = t0 + total

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Load known parent if available.
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        raw = np.nan_to_num(raw, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        raw = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if raw.size > 0:
            s = float(raw.sum())
            if s > 1e-8:
                p = raw / s
                sc, _, _ = score_p(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    best_seq = p.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                else:
                    best_seq = p.copy()
                    best_score = 2.0 * len(p)
                print("Parent n=" + str(len(best_seq)) +
                      " initial_score=" + repr(best_score))
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_seq is None:
        n_def = 6700
        best_seq = np.ones(n_def, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_def)
        best_score, _, _ = score_p(best_seq)
        print("Default start n=" + str(len(best_seq)) +
              " initial_score=" + repr(best_score))

    cand_ps = [best_seq]
    cand_scores = [best_score]

    # Fourier-space global exploration.
    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    t_fourier = min(180.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.11))
    if rem > 420.0 and t_fourier > 30.0:
        fours = fourier_search(t_fourier, seed=17)
        base_n = max(1000, len(best_seq))
        length_set = [base_n]
        for L in (base_n + 1500, base_n + 4000, base_n + 7000, 12000, 16000):
            if 3000 <= L <= 20000 and L not in length_set:
                length_set.append(L)

        for pN, _ in fours[:3]:
            for L in length_set:
                pL = interpolate_p(pN, L)
                sc, _, _ = score_p(pL)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    cand_ps.append(pL)
                    cand_scores.append(sc)

    # Keep a few diverse best candidates.
    if cand_ps:
        order = np.argsort(np.asarray(cand_scores, dtype=np.float64))
        kept_ps = []
        kept_sc = []
        for idx in order[:5]:
            if len(kept_ps) >= 5:
                break
            kept_ps.append(cand_ps[int(idx)])
            kept_sc.append(cand_scores[int(idx)])
        cand_ps = kept_ps
        cand_scores = kept_sc

    main_p = cand_ps[0]

    # Quick local polish before gradient search.
    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    t1 = min(210.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.17))
    if t1 > 25.0:
        p_out, _ = local_balance(main_p, t1, seed=11, verbose=False)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_out, "local0")

    # Main softmax optimization.
    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    t2 = min(520.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.30))
    if t2 > 50.0:
        p_out, _ = softmax_opt(main_p, t2, seed=21,
                               beta_start=5.0, beta_end=1000.0,
                               top_k_frac=0.02, lr0=0.06,
                               verbose=False)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_out, "softmain")

    # Detailed local polishing after gradient move.
    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    t3 = min(300.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.22))
    if t3 > 35.0:
        p_out, _ = local_balance(best_seq, t3, seed=22, verbose=False)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_out, "local1")

    # Optional second basin from the next-best initial candidate.
    if len(cand_ps) > 1:
        rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
        t4 = min(280.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.18))
        second_p = cand_ps[1]
        p_second_out = None
        if t4 > 45.0:
            p_second_out, _ = softmax_opt(second_p, t4, seed=31,
                                          beta_start=10.0, beta_end=800.0,
                                          top_k_frac=0.02, lr0=0.05,
                                          verbose=False)
            best_seq, best_score = try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_second_out, "soft2")

        rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
        t4b = min(150.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.10))
        if t4b > 20.0 and p_second_out is not None:
            p_out2, _ = local_balance(p_second_out, t4b, seed=32, verbose=False)
            best_seq, best_score = try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_out2, "local2")

    # Multi-length interpolation/polishing.
    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    n_best = len(best_seq)
    multi_lengths = []
    for L in (n_best + 1500, n_best + 4000, n_best + 7000, max(3000, n_best - 1500)):
        if 3000 <= L <= 20000 and L not in multi_lengths:
            multi_lengths.append(L)

    for mi, LL in enumerate(multi_lengths):
        rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 110.0:
            break
        p_int = interpolate_p(best_seq, LL)
        sc_int, _, _ = score_p(p_int)
        if np.isfinite(sc_int) and sc_int < best_score:
            best_seq = p_int
            best_score = sc_int
            print("  INTERP direct n=" + str(LL) + " score=" + repr(sc_int))

        rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
        tms = min(85.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.25))
        if tms > 18.0:
            p_out, _ = softmax_opt(p_int, tms, seed=41 + mi,
                                   beta_start=15.0, beta_end=700.0,
                                   top_k_frac=0.02, lr0=0.04,
                                   verbose=False)
            best_seq, best_score = try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_out,
                                                   "ml_soft_" + str(LL))

        rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
        tmpl = min(70.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.18))
        if tmpl > 15.0:
            p_out, _ = local_balance(best_seq, tmpl, seed=51 + mi, verbose=False)
            best_seq, best_score = try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_out,
                                                   "ml_loc_" + str(LL))

    # Final local surgery.
    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    tfinal = min(300.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.35))
    if tfinal > 30.0:
        p_out, _ = local_balance(best_seq, tfinal, seed=99, verbose=False)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_out, "final_local")

    # Final high-beta gradient burst.
    rem = max(0.0, end - time.monotonic())
    tfinal2 = min(110.0, max(0.0, rem * 0.30))
    if tfinal2 > 20.0:
        p_out, _ = softmax_opt(best_seq, tfinal2, seed=888,
                               beta_start=40.0, beta_end=1300.0,
                               top_k_frac=0.01, lr0=0.03,
                               verbose=False)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update_best(best_seq, best_score, p_out, "final_soft")

    # Final cleanup and return.
    if best_seq is None:
        n_fb = 6700
        best_seq = np.ones(n_fb, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_fb)
        best_score, _, _ = score_p(best_seq)

    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    best_seq = np.nan_to_num(best_seq, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_seq = np.maximum(best_seq, 0.0)
    ss = float(best_seq.sum())
    if ss < 1e-8:
        n_fb = 6700
        best_seq = np.ones(n_fb, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_fb)
        ss = float(n_fb)
    best_seq = best_seq / ss
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    final_score, _, _ = score_p(best_seq)
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    print("FINAL FFT-score=" + repr(final_score) +
          " n=" + str(len(best_seq)) +
          " elapsed=" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s")

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
